\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{忒瑞西阿斯}哦对，我是有一个消息，要带给一个女孩，或是女人。带给伊俄卡斯忒，还是阿尔刻提斯，或者是得伊阿尼拉，我也记不得是谁了，不过这事儿我还记着。你这孩子，别老拽我！{\kai （拿着手杖打男孩几下）}废物！放肆！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{233,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The Boy falls. Teiresias continues to beat him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 男孩摔倒在地，忒瑞西阿斯并没有住手。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{234,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Boy}
\ENstage{Screaming.}
Teiresias! Help! Help! King Admetus!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{男孩}{\kai （喊叫着）}忒瑞西阿斯！救命！救命！阿德墨托斯国王！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{235,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Surely, great Teiresias, the boy has not—\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}是啊，忒瑞西阿斯，这孩子也没有——\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{236,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
It was a small fault, Teiresias. I beg you spare the boy. He will learn.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}只是个小小的错误，忒瑞西阿斯。求你放过他吧，他知道错了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{237,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Teiresias}
\ENstage{Suddenly stopping and peering at Alcestis.}
Whatever your name is: Jocasta, Leda, Hermione—\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{忒瑞西阿斯}{\kai （突然停了下来，盯着阿尔刻提斯）}我不管你叫什么：伊俄卡斯忒，勒达，赫耳弥俄涅——\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{238,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}阿尔刻提斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{239,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Teiresias}
I had a message for some girl, but I have forgotten it. Or else I've delivered it already. That's it: I've delivered it. By thunder and lightning, by the holy tripod—what use is Delphi if men and women cannot learn to listen?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{忒瑞西阿斯}我是有个消息，要带给某个女孩，但我忘了，要么我已经说过了。嗯，我已经说过了，借着雷声与闪电，借着神圣的祭坛——如果人类学不会聆听，德尔斐要来有什么用？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{240,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Teiresias is following the Boy out of the gate, when Admetus takes some steps forward.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 忒瑞西阿斯跟在男孩后面走了出去，阿德墨托斯跟上几步。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{241,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
You said . . . you said there was peril, Teiresias?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}您说，您说会有一场危难是吗，忒瑞西阿斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{242,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Teiresias}
\ENstage{Half out of the gate.}
Of course there's peril, imbecile. When they\par
\ENstage{Brusque gesture upward.}
draw near it is always peril.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{忒瑞西阿斯}{\kai （马上要走出门外）}当然有危难了，呆子。每次他们{\kai （指着天空）}的来临就是一场危难。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{243,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
But my father said that Apollo has always loved Thessaly. . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}可我父亲说，阿波罗是爱着塞萨利的……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{244,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Teiresias}
Yes—love, love, love. Let them keep their love to themselves. Look at me: five—six hundred years old and pretty well loved by the gods and I am not allowed to die. If the gods didn't love men, we'd all be happy; and the other way round is true, too: if we men didn't love the gods, we'd all be happy.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{忒瑞西阿斯}是的，爱爱爱。大家还是自个爱自个吧。你看看我，五六百岁了，神很爱我，死都不让我死。如果神不爱人类，我们肯定都很快乐；换个角度同样成立：如果我们不爱神，我们肯定也很快乐。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{245,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Exit, with the Boy.\par
A bewildered pause.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 忒瑞西阿斯带着男孩下场。一个尴尬的停顿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{246,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Admetus collects himself and says in a more matter-of-fact, authoritative tone to the Four Herdsmen:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯整理了一下情绪，带着实事求是却又威严的语气对四个牧人开始讲话。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{247,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Again, you are welcome to Thessaly and to Pherai.\par
\ENstage{To the Watchman.}
See that they are well provided for.\par
\ENstage{Again to the Herdsmen.}
I am happy that you are to be guests at my wedding today.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}再次欢迎你们来到塞萨利，来到费莱。{\kai （对看门人）}请确保安顿好他们。{\kai （对牧人们）}很高兴你们也成为了今天婚礼的客人。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{248,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Admetus and all on the stage watch in confused awe as the Watchman guides them down the path to the spring. They pass Admetus with servile timidity; by the spring they stretch out, pass the wineskin from one to another; one promptly falls asleep. Admetus has not looked at Alcestis. Partly to her and partly to himself he says reflectively:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 看门人带领四个牧人沿着小路走向泉边，阿德墨托斯和舞台上的人们都好奇地看着。他们走过阿德墨托斯身边时，都像仆人一样怯生生的，到了泉边他们才放松下来，互相传递着酒囊，其中一人渐渐睡着了。阿德墨托斯并没有看向阿尔刻提斯，他对她说话，也是在对自己说。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{249,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I do not know what to think of these things. . . . I am a mere herdsman myself. Alcestis, there is great need of you in Pherai.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （继续）}我也不知道该怎么理解这些事儿……我自己也不过就是个牧人。阿尔刻提斯，费莱很需要你。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{250,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He stretches his hand out behind him. She, frozen in thought, does not take it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他伸出双手背到身后。她，沉思着愣在原地，毫无回应。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{251,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I must return to my guests.\par
\ENstage{With a last echo of his awe.}
I do not know what to think of these things.\par
\ENstage{Then with a smile.}
Alcestis, there is an old custom here in Thessaly that a bridegroom should not see the face of his bride until the evening of his wedding day. This has been said for many hundreds of years. Is there also such a custom at Iolcos?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}我得回去招呼客人了。{\kai （再次表达畏惧）}我真的不知道，该怎么理解这些事情。{\kai （忽然笑了一下）}阿尔刻提斯，在塞萨利有一个古老的习俗，直到婚礼当晚，新郎都不能见到新娘的面容。这个习俗已经流传好几百年了，在伊俄尔科斯也是这样吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{252,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Low.}
Yes, Admetus.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （低声）}是的，阿德墨托斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{253,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Passing her with youthful vigor, his hand shielding his face.}
Hereafter—by the God's gift—I may look upon your face until I die.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （活力满满地走过她身边，用手挡着自己的脸）}很快——感谢神的恩赐——我就可以一直望着你，直到我死去。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{254,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
At the door of the palace, he is arrested by a thought. Still shielding his face, he comes in slow deliberation to the point of the stage, overhanging the Herdsmen by the spring. After taking a deep breath of resolution, he says with unemphatic directness:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 走到王宫门口时，他忽然想起了什么。他仍然用手挡着自己的脸，缓缓地走到四个牧人在泉边所站立的地方。他下定决心般深深呼吸一口气，平静而直率地说道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{255,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Apollo, friend of my father and my ancestors and my land, I am a simple man, devout. I am not learned in piety. If, in ignorance, I blunder and fall short, may He who has been the friend of my house and my people forgive me. You have come on a day when I am the happiest of all men. Continue your favor to me and to my descendants. . . .\par
\ENstage{Slight pause.}
I am not skilled in speech. You can read all minds. Read what is in mine, or rather . . . yourself plant in my mind those wishes which only you can fulfill.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}阿波罗，我父亲、我祖先、我土地的朋友，我是一个虔诚的普通人，但我对真正的虔诚知晓不多。如果我不经意间有所冒犯，或是犯了错误，希望这位“祂”，我的家族、我的人民的朋友，能够原谅我。您在我人生最幸福的一天到来，也请继续赐福于我和我的后裔吧……{\kai （停顿）}我不太会说话，不过您能看透人心，请鉴察我的心，或者，您可以在我脑中埋下愿望，那些只有您能满足的愿望。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{256,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He turns and goes quickly into the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他转过身迅速地走进宫殿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{257,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Who has not ceased to keep her eyes on the Herdsmen, half in longing and half in doubt and repulsion—though they are now hidden from where she is standing, murmuring.}
Is Apollo there?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （一直在盯着牧人们看，带着期盼和渴望，又带着怀疑和抗拒——尽管此刻他们已经从她身前躲开了）}阿波罗在这儿吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{258,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Princess!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}公主！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{259,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
One of those? And could that old man have been Teiresias of Delphi—that broken, crazy old man?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}就是他们其中之一？那个老头就是德尔斐的忒瑞西阿斯——那个颤颤巍巍、疯疯癫癫的老头？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{260,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Really shocked; firmly.}
Do not doubt these things, Princess.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （被惊讶到，坚定地）}不要怀疑这些事情，公主。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{261,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{After taking a few steps toward them; in sudden resolve.}
Leave me alone with them.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （朝着牧人们走了几步，突然做了决定）}让我和他们单独待会儿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{262,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Aglaia makes a gesture to the Watchman and both go out.\par
During the following speech, though one Herdsman is asleep and snoring, the others are embarrassed by her presence. The wineskin is being passed around; they scarcely dare to raise their eyes to her.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚对看门人做了个手势，两人下场。在接下来的谈话中，一个牧人睡着了，还在打鼾，其他几个则在她面前十分窘迫。他们互相递着酒囊，都不敢抬头看她。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{263,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Are you here? I have spoken to you a thousand times—to the sky and the stars and the sun. And I have sent messages to Delphi. Are you now, truly, within the hearing of my voice?\par
\ENstage{Silence, broken by a snore and a grunt.}
Some say that you do not exist. Some say that the gods are far away; they are feasting on Olympus, or are asleep, or drunk. I have offered you my life. You know that I have wished to live only for you: to learn—to be taught by you—the meaning of our life.\par
\ENstage{No answer.}
Are we human beings to be left without any sign, any word? Are we abandoned?\par
\ENstage{She waits another second above the embarrassed silence of the Herdsmen, then turns toward the palace, and says to herself bitterly:}
Then we must find our way by ourselves . . . and life is a meaningless grasping at this and that; it is a passionate nonsense. . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}您在这儿吗？我已经向您呼求成百上千次了——向着天空、群星、太阳。我向德尔斐献上了数不清的祷告。您现在是否，确确实实，听到了我的声音？{\kai （静场，突然被鼾声打断）}有人说，您不存在。有人说，神明都在很远的地方，他们在奥林匹斯山上欢宴，饮酒，沉睡。我已经把生命献给了您。您知道，我希望只为您而活：去学习——由您来教导——生命的意义。{\kai （没有回应）}人类是不是无法得到启示、教训？我们是不是已经被抛弃了？{\kai （她等待着，场上只有牧人们尴尬的沉默。她转身向王宫走去，痛苦地自言自语）}那么我们只能自己寻找出路了……生命不过是追寻这些那些过后的虚无，是一种充满激情的空虚……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{264,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The First Herdsman—the dirtiest, most insignificant of the four—rises. He touches his cap in humble embarrassment and says:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 第一个牧人——最脏的，四人中最不起眼的——站了起来。他谦卑而害羞地摸了摸自己的帽檐，说道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{265,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
Princess, did that old man say that there was a god among us? Did I hear him say that? The God Apollo? Then, lady, I am as surprised as you are. Lady, for thirty days we four have walked all across Greece. We have drunk from the same wineskin; we have put our hands in the same dish; we have slept by the same fire. If there had been a god among us, would I not have known it?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}公主，那个老头说我们之中有一个是神？他确实这么说了？阿波罗？如果这样，夫人，我和您一样惊讶。夫人，我们四人在过去三十天穿越整个希腊，用同一个酒囊饮酒，用同一个碟子吃饭，围着同一个火堆睡觉。如果我们之中真有一位神明，我怎么会没发现呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{266,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis, in hope and revulsion, has taken several steps toward him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯带着希望和厌恶，朝他走了几步。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{267,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
By all I value, lady, I swear we are just ordinary herdsmen. Ignorant herdsmen. But . . . but one thing I will say, lady: we are not quite ordinary herdsmen. Why, that fellow there—the one that's snoring: there's no illness he cannot cure. Snakebite or a broken back. Yet I know that he is not a god, Princess. And that fellow beside him, that one!\par
\ENstage{He goes forward and kicks the Herdsman.}
Can't you stop drinking while the princess is looking at you? He never loses his way. In the darkest night he knows his north from his south and his east from his west. Oh, it's wonderful. Yet I know well that he's not the god of the sun.\par
\ENstage{Adding under his breath:}
Besides, his habits are filthy, are filthy.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}照我看，夫人，我发誓我们只是普通的牧人罢了，无知的牧人。但……但是有件事值得一提，夫人，我们也不是平庸的牧人。怎么说呢？那边那个家伙——在打鼾的那个家伙，没有什么疾病是他治不好的，被蛇咬伤或是背部骨折，不过我知道他不是神，公主。他旁边的那个家伙，那个！{\kai （他走过去踢了那个牧人一脚）}你能不能别喝了？公主看着你呢。他从来不会迷路，即使在最黑的夜里，他也能准确分辨东西南北，很神奇。不过我知道他也不是太阳之神。{\kai （小声说道）}另外，他有些很下流的习惯，很下流。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{268,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Barely breathing it.}
And that one?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （毫不在意）}那个呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{269,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
That man? He's our singer.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}那个？他是我们的歌手。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{270,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Ah!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}啊！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{271,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
Believe me, when he plays the lyre and sings—oh, Princess! It is true that at times I have said to myself, ``Surely this is a god.'' He can fill us to the brim with joy or sadness when we have no reason at all to be joyful or sad. He can make the memory of love more sweet than love itself. But, Princess, he is no god.\par
\ENstage{As though she had contradicted him; with sudden argumentative energy.}
How can he be a god when he's in misery all the time and drinking himself to death? Killing himself, you might say, before our own eyes. The gods don't hate themselves, Princess.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}我跟您说，当他弹着竖琴唱起歌时——哦，公主！每当那时我都对自己说“这是神啊”。即便没有什么值得快乐或忧伤的事情发生，他的音乐也会让你感到满溢的欢喜和忧愁。他可以使爱的记忆变得比爱本身更加甜蜜，但是公主，他不是神。{\kai （她早就想反驳他，想要争辩一番）}一个总是一副苦脸，随时要把自己喝死的人怎么会是神呢？他在慢性自杀，您知道的，在我们眼皮底下。神明们可不憎恨自己，公主。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{272,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
And you?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}你呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{273,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
I? \textit{I}, Apollo? Not only am I not Apollo, but I'm not ready to believe that Apollo is here.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}我？我是阿波罗？我不但不是阿波罗，我也不相信阿波罗在这儿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{274,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Teiresias . . . Teiresias said . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}忒瑞西阿斯，忒瑞西阿斯说了……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{275,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
Was that Teiresias—that half-witted, crumbling old man? Can they find no better messenger than that? Can't they say what they have to say in any clearer way than this?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}那个忒瑞西阿斯——那个路都走不稳还稀里糊涂的老头？还能找到比他更差劲的送信人吗？还能把神谕说得再不清不楚一点吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{276,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Again Alcestis turns toward the palace; then turns toward the Herdsman and says, as though talking to herself:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯转身向王宫走去。她又转过身来看着牧人，像是在对自己说话。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{277,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Then we are indeed miserable. Not only because we have no aid, but because we are cheated with the hope that we might have aid. . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}这样说来，我们真的很可悲。我们不仅得不到帮助，还傻傻地相信我们可能会得到一些帮助。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{278,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Taking more steps onto the stage.}
But if they did exist, these gods, how would they speak to us? In what language would they talk to us? Compared to them, we are diseased and dying and deaf and blind and as busy as clowns. Why, there are some who even say that they love us. Could you understand that? What kind of love is that, Princess, when there is so great a gulf between the lovers?\par
\ENstage{He starts to return to his place in the path.}
That would be an unhappy love, no doubt about that.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （向前走了几步到舞台上）}但如果神明们真的存在，他们会如何对我们说话呢？他们会使用哪种语言？和他们比起来，我们不过是病态的、将死的、又聋又瞎还忙得团团转的小丑。为什么有人会觉得神爱我们。您想得通吗？当爱人们之间隔着如此巨大的鸿沟，公主，这算哪门子爱啊？{\kai （他慢慢走回自己的位置）}这种爱不会快乐，简直不用怀疑。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{279,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Earnestly and sharply.}
No, not unhappy!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （恳切而尖锐地）}不，谁说不会快乐！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{280,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{With equal spirit.}
Yes. For if they showed themselves to us in their glory, it would kill us.\par
\ENstage{Pause.}
I did have an idea this morning: maybe there is another way—a way to bridge that gulf, I mean. Maybe they can find a way to bring those they love up—up nearer to them. If Teiresias is right, Apollo is here in Thessaly. Now, maybe that foolish old man got his message wrong. Maybe he was supposed to say that Apollo is here divided up among many people—us four herdsmen and others! Take Admetus, for example. I've only seen him for a few hours. I must confess, Princess, at first I was very disappointed in Admetus. There's nothing very extraordinary about him. Did you ever see Hercules, Princess?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （带着同样的情绪）}是。如果他真让我们看到他的荣光，那会杀死我们的。{\kai （停顿）}对于今早发生的事，我有些想法，或许有别的方法，去填补这个鸿沟。或许神明们会找到一种方法，让他们所爱之人变得更好，更接近他们。如果忒瑞西阿斯说得没错，那阿波罗确实在塞萨利。也或许，那个老糊涂捎错了信儿，或许信息本来是，阿波罗在这里，分散在了每一个人身上——我们四个牧人，还有其他人！比如说阿德墨托斯，我才见过他几小时，我必须要说，公主，一开始，我对阿德墨托斯非常失望，他看上去一点也没有什么不凡之处。您见过赫拉克勒斯吧，公主？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{281,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Nods her head slightly.}
Yes.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （轻轻点了点头）}见过。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{282,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Suddenly recollecting.}
Ah, yes. He sought your hand. There's a man! Hercules, son of Zeus and Alcmene. And you can see it at once. I've seen a dozen better men than Admetus. But . . . slowly I began to see that King Admetus has something that all those other heroes haven't got. . . . The world changes; it changes slowly. What good would this world be, Princess, unless new kinds of men came into it—and new kinds of women?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （突然回想起来）}啊，对。他追求过您。很有个样儿！赫拉克勒斯，宙斯与阿尔克墨涅之子，您一眼就能看出来。我见过不少比阿德墨托斯更强的人，但渐渐地，我发现阿德墨托斯身上有一些别的英雄没有的东西……这个世界在改变，慢慢地改变。除非有不一样的男人、女人们出现，公主，这个世界怎么会变得更好呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{283,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Enter Admetus, wearing on his right shoulder a light-blue cloak like Apollo's. He stands watching from the top step.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯上场，右肩上披着像阿波罗一样的浅蓝色的斗篷，他站在最上面一级台阶观望着。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{284,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
And wouldn't that be, maybe, the way those unhappy lovers\par
\ENstage{He points upward.}
would try to throw a bridge across the gulf I was talking about?\par
\ENstage{He becomes aware of Admetus. Obsequiously bowing his head, and pulling his forelock, murmuring.}
We wish you all happiness . . . many years.\par
\ENstage{He goes back to his place.}}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}这不就是，或许，就是那些伤感的爱人们，{\kai （指指天空）}在想方设法，在我刚说的鸿沟上面搭建的一座桥梁？{\kai （意识到阿德墨托斯的出现，谦卑地低下头，用手触摸着前额，默念着）}我们祝您幸福，长长久久。{\kai （回到自己的位置上）}\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{285,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The sun is setting. The sky behind the palace roof is filled with color.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 太阳渐渐落山，宫殿背后的天空被染上了色彩。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{286,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{With set face.}
Aglaia has told me of your wish to leave, Princess Alcestis. You are, of course, free to go. There are no constraints here. There are no slaves in Thessaly, Princess—not even its queen. I have just given orders that your drivers and your maids be prepared for the journey.\par
\ENstage{He takes one step down.}
Before you leave, I wish to say one thing. I do not say this in order to win your pity, nor to dissuade you from what you have planned to do. I say this because you and I are not children; and we should not conceal from one another what is in our hearts.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （板着脸）}阿尔刻提斯公主，阿格莱亚已经告知了我您离开的意愿。您，当然有离开的自由，我们不会强留。塞萨利没有奴隶，公主，它的王后也不会成为奴隶。我已经命令您的马夫和女仆为您打点好旅途上需要的一切。{\kai （向下走一步）}在您离开之前，我想说一件事，说这些不是为了让您可怜我，也没有劝您回头的打算。我说这些话是因为我们都是成年人了，对心中所想的事情不必掩藏。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{287,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENstage{Slight pause.}
It is still a great wonder to me that I was able to yoke the lion and the boar. But I am in no doubt as to \textit{why} I was able to do it: I loved you. I shall never see you again. I shall never marry. And I shall never be the same. From now on I shall know that there is something wrong and false in this world into which we have been born. I am an ordinary man, but the love that filled me is not an ordinary love. When such love is not met by a love in return, then life is itself a deception. And it is best that men live at random, as best they may. For justice . . . and honor . . . and love are just things we invent for a short time, as suits the moment. May you have a good journey, Princess Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup {\kai （轻微的停顿）}能够同时驾驭狮子和野猪，这件事情直到现在都让我觉得不可思议。但为什么能做到这一点，原因我毫不怀疑：我爱您。我今后永远都见不到您了，我永远不会再娶妻，我也永远不会是现在的我。从今天开始，我不得不意识到，这个世界总是有所残缺的。我是一个普通的人，但那充满在我心中的爱，不是普通的爱。如果这样的爱都得不到回应，那生命本身就成了一场谎言。人们只需要随波逐流地生活着，所谓正义、荣誉、爱，不过是短暂的、瞬间的一种妄想。愿您旅途一切平安，阿尔刻提斯公主。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{288,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis has been standing with raised head but lowered eyes. She now puts out her hand.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯站在那里，慢慢抬起头，但仍不抬眼。她伸出了她的手。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{289,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Admetus.\par
\ENstage{Slight pause.}
Admetus, ask me again to marry you.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}阿德墨托斯。{\kai （轻微停顿）}阿德墨托斯，再向我求一次婚吧。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{290,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He takes one quick step toward her; she again puts out her hand quickly to stop him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯一个箭步向阿尔刻提斯走去，她再次伸出手，让他停下。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{291,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup Ask me to love all the things that you love . . . and to be the queen of your Thessaly. Ask me in pain to bear you children. To walk beside you at the great festivals. To comfort you when you are despairing. To make sure that when you return from a journey the water for your bath will be hot, and that your house, Admetus, will be as well ordered as your mind. To live for you and for your children and for your people—to live for you as though every moment I were ready to die for you . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 让我去爱你所爱，成为你的塞萨利的王后。让我为你生儿育女，即使经历痛苦，让我在盛大的宴会上挽着你走过，让我在你失意时安慰你，让我在你远行归来时准备好沐浴的热水，让我好好照料你的家，阿德墨托斯，像你所期待的一样。让我为你、你的孩子、你的人民而生活——让我每一刻都为你活着，每一刻都可以为你死去。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{292,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Joyously and loudly.}
No—no Alcestis! It is I . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （开心地，大声地）}不，不，阿尔刻提斯！应该是我……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{293,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He takes her outstretched hand. They do not embrace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他拉起她伸出的手，他们并没有拥抱。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{294,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup Alcestis, will you be the wife of Admetus, King of Thessaly?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 阿尔刻提斯，你是否愿意成为塞萨利国王——阿德墨托斯的妻子？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{295,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
With my whole self, Admetus . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}我愿交出我的所有，阿德墨托斯……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{296,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
They go into the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他们走进宫殿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{297,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The same scene, twelve years later.\par
Again first dawn. The same Watchman comes around the palace.\par
He does not shake his rattle. He speaks softly, slowly, and dejectedly.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 同一场景，十二年之后。仍然是黎明第一道光，仍然是同一个看门人绕过宫殿走出来。他并没有敲鼓，轻声而缓慢地说着话，情绪十分低落。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{298,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
The watch before dawn . . . the palace of Admetus the Hospitable, King of Thessaly, rich in horses.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}黎明时分，照例巡查。热情诚挚的阿德墨托斯，塞萨利之王，千金万马的拥有者。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{299,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENstage{He stands a moment, his eyes on the ground; then to the audience.}
And all is as bad as it possibly could be . . . as it possibly could be.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup {\kai （低着头原地站了一会儿，开始对观众说话）}事情要多糟糕有多糟糕，非常糟糕。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{300,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENstage{He goes to the gate, which is ajar, and talks to a whispering crowd outside.}
No, my friends. There is no news. The King is still alive; he has lived through another night. The Queen is sitting by his bed. She is holding his hand. . . . No news. No change. . . . Take that dog away; we must have no barking. Yes, the King drank some ox blood mixed with wine.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup {\kai （门半掩着，他走到右边门口，对外面的人们轻声说着）}没有，朋友们，没什么新消息。国王还活着，他又撑过了一晚，王后一直守着他，紧紧握着他的手。没有新消息，没什么变化……把那条狗牵走，我们这儿需要安静。对，国王喝了点掺着公牛血的酒，\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{301,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENstage{He returns to the center of the stage and addresses the audience.}
Our King is at the point of death, there is no doubt about it. You remember those herdsmen, twelve years ago, that Teiresias brought here, saying that one of them was Apollo—and that Apollo would live here as a servant for a year? You remember all that?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup {\kai （走回到场中央，对观众说道）}但是不得不承认，国王已经处在死亡边缘。你们应该记得那几个牧人，十二年前，忒瑞西阿斯带他们到这儿来，说他们中间有一个人是阿波罗——阿波罗会作为仆人在这儿生活一年，你们还记得吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{302,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup Well, at the end of the year did they go away? No; all four of them are still here. Was Apollo in one of them? Nobody knows. Don't try to think about it; you'll lose your senses if you try to understand things like that. But, friends, that first year was a wonderful year. I cannot explain this to you: it was no more or less prosperous than other years. To a visitor, to a passer-by, everything would have appeared the same. But to us who were living it, everything was different. The facts of our human life never change; it is our way of seeing them that changes. Apollo was certainly here.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 嗯，一年结束之后他们走了吗？没有，他们四个还待在这儿。阿波罗确实是这四人之一？没人知道。别想这些事了，这种事想多了会变成神经病的。不过朋友们，那一年确实很神奇。我没法解释清楚：对于国家繁荣程度来说，那一年和其他年份没太大区别。对于来访的，路过的人来说，一切看起来都是老样子。但对我们这些生活在其中的人来说，一切都不一样了。人类生活的本质从没改变过，是我们看待生活的眼光发生了改变。阿波罗确实就在这里。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{303,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENstage{Pause.}
What? How did King Admetus come by his illness? Well, as I say, those four herdsmen stayed on. They were good fellows and good workers—though they talked south-country Greek. But two of them—eh!—drank their wine without water. And one evening at sunset time those two were sitting drinking—there, right there!—and a quarrel arose between them. There was shouting and chasing about, and King Admetus came out to put a stop to it. Then—oh, my friends—one of the herdsmen whipped out his knife and stabbed King Admetus by mistake, stabbed him from here\par
\ENstage{Points to his throat.}
right down his side to the waist. You could put your hand in it. That was weeks ago. The wound didn't heal. It got angry and boiled and watered and boiled and watered, and now the King must surely die. No one's wanted to punish the herdsman whose body held Apollo—you see? And the crime may have been Apollo's will—try to think that through! And that herdsman's outside the gate now—is always there—flat on the ground, flat on his face, wishing he were dead.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup {\kai （停顿）}嗯？阿德墨托斯国王怎么生病了？我不是说了吗，那四个牧人留了下来。他们是好人，好工人——虽然他们操着一口希腊南方的乡村口音。其中还有两人——呃！嗜酒如命。有一天晚上，太阳落山的时候他们就坐在那儿喝酒，那儿，就在那儿，一边喝一边开始吵架。他们叫着嚷着，阿德墨托斯国王出来想要制止。结果，天哪，其中一个牧人拿着刀，一不小心刺到了国王，从这儿{\kai （指着喉咙）}一刀划到腰上。伤口大得能把手伸进去。这已经是几星期前的事儿了，但伤口还是没见好。伤口发炎然后结痂，又流水又结痂的，国王看来是难逃此劫了。没人打算怪罪那个牧人，因为他可能就是阿波罗啊——你明白吗？这或许就是阿波罗的旨意——想想看！现在那个牧人就在门外——他一直在那儿——跪在地上，把脸埋在地上，希望死的是他自己。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{304,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
There is a rising murmur of excited voices at the gate.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 门边有人在嘀咕，声音越来越大，听上去很激动。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{305,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Now what's all that noise about?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}这又是怎么了？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{306,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Aglaia comes hurriedly out of the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚急匆匆地从宫殿里出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{307,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
What's all that noise? Watchman, how can you allow that noise?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}出什么事了？看门人，这么吵闹你也不管管？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{308,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
\ENstage{Hobbling to the gate.}
They've only just begun it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}{\kai （一瘸一拐地朝门口走去）}我也刚听见。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{309,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{At the gate.}
What's all this cackling about? Don't you know you must be quiet?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （站在门边）}你们啰唆个不停在说什么？不知道我们这儿需要安静吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{310,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Have you no sense? Have you no hearts? What? What?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}你们讲不讲理啊？你们有没有心肝啊？什么？什么？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{311,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
What? What messenger? Tell him to go around to the front of the palace and to go quietly. What messenger? Where's he from?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}什么？什么信使？告诉他去宫殿前门，一定要安静。什么信使？从哪儿来的？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{312,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Enter Alcestis, from the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯上场，她正从宫殿里走出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{313,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
There must be no noise. There must be no noise. Aglaia, how can you allow this noise?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}我们这儿要安静，要安静。阿格莱亚，这么吵闹你也不管管吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{314,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Queen Alcestis, they say that a messenger has come.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}阿尔刻提斯王后，他们说来了一位信使。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{315,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
What messenger?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}什么信使？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{316,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Has received through the gate a small rectangular leaf of gold, which he gives to Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 有人从门缝递给他一小片长方形的金叶子，他转交给阿尔刻提斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{317,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
They are saying that a messenger came during the night and left this.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}他们说夜里来了一位信使，留下了这个。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{318,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis stands looking at it in her hand. The Watchman leans over and examines it in her hand and continues talking officiously.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯拿在手里看了看，看门人也凑上去仔细看了看，严肃地说道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{319,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
It is of gold, lady. See, those are the signs—that is the writing—of the Southland. There is the sun . . . and the tripod . . . and the laurel. . . . Queen Alcestis, it is from Delphi. It is a message from Delphi!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}这是金的，夫人。您看，那些标志——是南方的文字。那是太阳——祭坛，还有桂冠……阿尔刻提斯王后，这是从德尔斐送来的，这是从德尔斐送来的神谕！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{320,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis suddenly performs the ritual: she places the leaf first to her forehead, then on her heart, then on her lips.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯立刻行起了仪式：她把叶子放到额前，然后放到胸前，然后放到唇边。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{321,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Where is the messenger?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}信使在哪里？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{322,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The Watchman goes to the gate—now slightly open—and holds a whispered colloquy.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 看门人走到此刻微微开着的门边，小声与外面的人交谈起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{323,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
\ENstage{Turning back toward Alcestis.}
He went away—hours ago, they say.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}{\kai （转身对阿尔刻提斯）}他们说，他几个小时之前走了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{324,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Who can read this writing? Watchman, go call those four herdsmen!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}谁能看懂这些字？看门人，去把那四个牧人叫来！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{325,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
They are here, lady—all four of them.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}他们就在这儿，夫人，四个人都在这儿了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{326,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Again a busy colloquy at the gate. It opens further to admit the four Herdsmen. The Herdsman who held the long conversation with Alcestis in Act I flings himself on the ground, face downward, holding his head in his hands.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 门口又传来说话的声音。看门人开门让四个牧人进来。第一幕曾与阿尔刻提斯长谈过的那个牧人俯伏在地上，脸朝着地面，双手抱着自己的头。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{327,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Can one of you read the writing of the Southland?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}你们有人认识南方文字吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{328,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Raising his head.}
I can . . . a little, Queen Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （抬起头）}我认识，一点，阿尔刻提斯王后。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{329,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Then stand. Stand up.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}你先起来。站起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{330,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He rises. She puts the leaf in his hand. He looks at it a moment, then puts it to his forehead, heart, and lips; and says in awe:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他站了起来，她把叶子放在他手里，他看了一下，然后把叶子依次放在自己额前、胸前和唇边，敬畏地说道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{331,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
It is from the temple of Apollo at Delphi.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}这是从德尔斐的阿波罗神殿送来的信息。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{332,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Some Townspeople have pressed through the gate. The Herdsman starts reading with great difficulty.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 几个镇上的人从门口挤了进来，牧人艰难地辨认并读出文字。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{333,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
``Peace . . . and long life . . . to Admetus the Hospitable, King of Thessaly, rich in horses.''\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}平安……与长寿……归于热情诚挚的阿德墨托斯，塞萨利之王，千金万马的拥有者。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{334,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Long life?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}长寿？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{335,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
``Peace and long life to Admetus the Hospitable—''\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}平安与长寿归于热情诚挚的阿德墨托斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{336,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Stop.\par
\ENstage{To the Watchman.}
Close the gate.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}等等。{\kai （对看门人）}把门关起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{337,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The Watchman pushes the Townspeople off the stage, and after a glance at Alcestis, also drives off the three other Herdsmen. He closes the gate. Alcestis returns to the reader, who has been trying to decipher the text.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 看门人把镇上的人赶下台去，他看了阿尔刻提斯一眼，把其他三个牧人也叫了下去，然后关上门。阿尔刻提斯走到牧人身旁，他正在努力辨认叶子上的字。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{338,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
``King Admetus . . . \textit{not} . . . will not . . . die.''\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}“阿德墨托斯国王，不，不会，死。”\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{339,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Great is Apollo! Great is Apollo!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}伟大的阿波罗！伟大的阿波罗！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{340,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
``Will not die . . . if . . . if . . . because . . . no, if . . . another . . . if someone else . . . if a second person . . . desires . . .\par
\ENstage{He points to his heart.}
wishes . . . longs . . . desires to die . . .'' Lady, I do not know this last word.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}“不会死，如果，如果，因为，不，如果，另一个，如果另一个人，第二个人，想要，{\kai （他指了指自己的心）}希望，渴望，想要去死。”夫人，我不认识最后一个字。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{341,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
I know it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}我明白了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{342,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Struggling, finds it.}
``In his place . . . in his stead.'' Great is Apollo!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （费力地，终于想到）}“在他的位置，代替他。”伟大的阿波罗！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{343,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
What does it say? What does it say?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}这是什么意思？什么意思？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{344,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
I. I shall die.\par
\ENstage{Starting left.}
My sword, where is my sword?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}我。我应该去死。{\kai （走开）}我的剑，我的剑呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{345,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Getting the idea.}
No.\par
\ENstage{Strongly, to the Watchman.}
No. This message is for me. I was there when he was born: I shall die for him. I shall throw myself in the river.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （明白过来）}不。{\kai （对看门人坚决地说道）}不，这是给我的信息。我是看着他出生的，我也应该为了他去死。我要去投河自尽。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{346,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
\ENstage{Returning; to Aglaia.}
This is not for a woman to do.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}{\kai （回答阿格莱亚）}这不是女人该干的事情。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{347,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Kneeling, his fists pressed against his eyes in concentration.}
I have begun to die already.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （跪下，他用双拳捂住自己的眼睛）}我早就想要去死了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{348,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Queen Alcestis, tell me . . . I must do it properly: how does one die for King Admetus?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}阿尔刻提斯王后，请吩咐吧，我一定办到。要怎么为阿德墨托斯国王去死？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
