\immediate\write\metrics{349,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
\ENstage{Self-importantly, placing himself between Aglaia and Alcestis.}
Give your orders to me, Queen Alcestis. It is very clear that this message was meant for me.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}{\kai （自告奋勇，站在阿格莱亚和阿尔刻提斯中间）}阿尔刻提斯王后，下命令给我吧。很明显这个信息就是给我的。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{350,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Again throwing himself full length on the ground.}
I struck him. I am to blame for all!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （再次全身俯伏在地）}是我伤害了他，这是我应得的！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{351,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Who has waited, motionless, for silence.}
How would you die in his stead?\par
\ENstage{Pause.}
Do you think it is enough to fling yourself into the river—or to run a sword through your heart?\par
\ENstage{Pause.}
No—the gods do not ask what is easy of us, but what is difficult.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （一直一动不动，沉默着没有说话）}你们怎么能替他去死呢？{\kai （停顿）}你们以为把自己扔进河里就行了吗？一刀刺进心里就行了吗？不，神明们不会让一切来得这么容易。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{352,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Sobbing.}
Queen Alcestis, how does one die for King Admetus?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （啜泣着）}阿尔刻提斯王后，要怎么为阿德墨托斯国王去死？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{353,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
I know. I know.\par
\ENstage{He turns and starts to go to the gate.}
Peace and long life to King Admetus!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}我知道，我知道。{\kai （转身向门口走去）}平安与长寿归于阿德墨托斯国王！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{354,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{To the Herdsman.}
Wait!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （对牧人）}站住！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{355,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
They all watch her. She says quietly:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 大家都看着她，她平静地说道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{356,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Aglaia, go and lay out the dress in which I was married.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}阿格莱亚，去把我结婚时穿的那条裙子拿出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{357,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Stares at her in sudden realization; horrified whisper.}
No. No . . . you must not . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （突然明白过来，盯着她，惊恐地小声说）}不，不，您不能……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{358,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Tell the children I am coming to see them. Tell the King the sun is warm here. He must come out and sit in it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}告诉孩子们我等下过来看他们。告诉国王外面太阳很暖和，他应该出来晒晒太阳。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{359,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Sudden clamor from the others.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 大家彼此争论起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{360,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
No. No, Queen Alcestis. We are old. Our lives are over.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}不，不，阿尔刻提斯王后。我们已经老了，我们活得差不多了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{361,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Lady, look at me—I am an old man.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}夫人，您看看我——我是个老人。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{362,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
You cannot do this. He would not wish it. You are queen; you are mother.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}您不能这么做，他也不会同意的。您是王后，您是孩子们的母亲。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{363,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Restraining their noise by voice and gesture.}
First! First I order you to say nothing of this to anyone. Aglaia, do you hear me?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （同时用言语和手势制止他们的发言）}首先！首先我命令你们，这件事情不许对任何人说起。阿格莱亚，你听到了吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{364,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, Queen Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}是的，阿尔刻提斯王后。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{365,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Watchman!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}看门人！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{366,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Yes, Queen Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}是的，阿尔刻提斯王后。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{367,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
To no one—until tomorrow. No matter what takes place here, you will show no surprise, no grief. Now leave me alone with this man.\par
\ENstage{Pointing to the Herdsman.}}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}直到明天，不能对任何人说。无论这里发生了什么，你们都不许惊讶，不许悲伤。现在让我和他单独待一会儿。{\kai （指着牧人）}\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{368,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Aglaia goes into the palace; the Watchman out left.\par
The Herdsman stands with one fist on his forehead in concentration, and suddenly cries:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚走进宫殿，看门人下场。牧人站在那里，一只手放在额前沉思着，突然喊了起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{369,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
King Admetus—rise up! Rise up!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}阿德墨托斯国王——起来吧！起来吧！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{370,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
You cannot save him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}你救不了他的。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{371,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
I have begun to die already.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}我早就想要去死了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{372,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Yes, maybe you could do it. But you would do it imperfectly. You wish to die, yes . . . not for love of Admetus, but to lift the burden of that crime from off your heart. This is work of love, Herdsman; not work of expiation, but of love.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}是，或许你能做到，但你没法把这件事完全做好。你希望去死，是的，但不是因为你爱阿德墨托斯，只是为了让心中的罪恶感得到解脱。这件事应该是为了爱而做，不是为了赎罪，是为了爱。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{373,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Almost angrily.}
I, too, love Admetus.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （有些恼怒）}我，也爱着阿德墨托斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{374,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Who does not love Admetus? But your death would be a small death. You long to die: I dread, fear, hate to die. I must die from Admetus—\par
\ENstage{She looks upward.}
from this sunlight. Only so will he be restored. Can you give him that?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}有谁不爱他呢？但你的死太轻微了。你渴望去死，而我，恐惧、害怕、憎恶死亡。我必须为了阿德墨托斯而死，{\kai （望着天空）}在这日光下。只有这样，他才能够重生。你能为他做到这些吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{375,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He is silent. She continues, as though talking to herself.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 牧人沉默了，她继续自言自语般地说着。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{376,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup I know now what I have to do and how to do it. But I do not know why . . . why this has been asked of me. You . . . you can help me to understand why I must die.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 我现在知道了要做什么，该怎么做，但我不明白为什么……为什么是我。你……你能让我明白，为什么我必须死去。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{377,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
I?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}我？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{378,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Yes, you—who came from Delphi; in whom Apollo—\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}是的，你，你从德尔斐来，阿波罗就在你……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{379,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{In sincere repudiation.}
Lady . . . Princess, I told you—again and again. If Apollo was in us herdsmen, he was not in me!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （真诚地回绝）}夫人，公主，我告诉过您很多很多次了。就算阿波罗在这儿，也绝对和我没关系。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{380,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Softly and lightly.}
You made clear to me that Apollo willed my marriage.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （温和而轻柔地）}是你让我明白了，我的婚姻是阿波罗的旨意。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{381,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Still almost angrily.}
No. No. You asked me questions. I answered as any man would answer. You know well that I am an ordinary man.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （有些恼怒）}不，不。您当时问了问题，我像其他人一样回答您。您知道，我不过是个普普通通的人。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{382,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis turns in suffering frustration toward the palace door; then turns back again to the Herdsman.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯转过身去，面对宫殿大门，沮丧至极，片刻又转过身来对牧人说话。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{383,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Then speak again as an ordinary man, and tell me why Apollo asks me to die.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}那就再次作为一个普普通通的人回答我的问题，告诉我为什么阿波罗要让我死。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{384,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
\ENstage{Still with a touch of anger.}
Then as an ordinary man I answer you—as many an ordinary man would: Delphi has said that one of us must die. I am ready to die. Why should I try to understand it?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}{\kai （仍然带着一些恼怒）}那么作为一个普普通通的人，我现在回答您——像其他人一样回答您：德尔斐的旨意说，我们其中一人必须去死，我也做好准备去死了，为什么一定要明白为什么呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{385,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
But if we do not understand, our lives are little better than those of the animals.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}如果不明白原因，那我们的生命和动物有什么区别？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{386,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Herdsman}
No! Princess, to understand means to see the whole of a thing. Do we men ever see the whole of a story, the end of a story? If you let me die now for Admetus, I would not know what followed after my death, but I would die willingly. For I have always seen that there are two kinds of death: one which is an end; and one which is a going forward, which is big with what follows after it. And I would know that a death which had been laid on me by Delphi would be a death which led on to something. For if the gods exist, that is their sign: that whatever they do is an unfolding—a part of something larger than we can see. Let me die this death, Princess—for it would save me from that other death which I dread and which all men dread: the mere ceasing to be; the dust in the grave.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{牧人}不！公主，明白，意味着您要看到整个事情。我们人类有谁看到过整个故事呢，有谁看到过故事的结局呢？如果您让我为阿德墨托斯去死，我不知道等我死后会发生什么，但是我心甘情愿。因为我一直明白这世上有两种死亡：一种是终局；另一种是延续，因着随之而来的一切而有了分量。我知道，如果这死亡的命运是从德尔斐赐下给我的，那这死就不会是白白的。如果众神真的存在，我想这就是他们的启示：众神所做的一切还未曾揭示——而我们看到的只是那广大之中极小的一部分。让我接受这死亡吧，公主。因为这能够拯救我脱离另外一种死亡，那正是我所恐惧的，也是所有人恐惧的——死，不过是生命的停止，坟墓中的尘埃。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{387,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis has received his words as full answer and solution. Her mood changes; she says lightly and quickly:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯在他的言辞中找到了所有答案。她的情绪有了变化，轻声而迅速地说道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{388,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
No, Herdsman, live—live for Admetus, for me, and for my children. Are you not the friend of Epimenes—he who hates you now because you struck his father? You have almost broken his heart. He thought you were his friend—you who taught him to swim and to fish. Before I go I shall tell Epimenes that you have done me a great kindness.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}不，牧人，活下去——为了阿德墨托斯，为了我，为了我的孩子们活下去。你是伊皮伊尼兹的朋友呢，虽然因为你伤害了他父亲，他现在恨你，你让他伤心坏了。他一直把你当作好朋友，是你教会了他游泳、钓鱼。在离开之前，我会告诉伊皮伊尼兹，你给了我多么大的恩惠。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{389,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
In silence the Herdsman goes out the gate.\par
Alcestis again makes the ritual gesture with the gold leaf—forehead, heart, lips. Enter Aglaia from the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 牧人默默走了出去，阿尔刻提斯拿金叶子又行了一次仪式：额前，胸前，唇边。阿格莱亚从宫殿里走了出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{390,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{With lowered head.}
The dress is ready, Queen Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （低着头）}裙子已经准备好了，阿尔刻提斯王后。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{391,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Aglaia, for what I have to do now I cannot—I must not—see the children. Their heads smell so sweet. Do you understand?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}阿格莱亚，我现在要做这件事，所以我不能，绝不能去见孩子们。他们的小脑瓜闻起来太美好了。你明白吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{392,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, Queen Alcestis.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}明白，阿尔刻提斯王后。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{393,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
You are to tell Epimenes—from me—that he is to go to the herdsman who struck his father . . . that he is to forgive him, and to thank him for a great kindness which he has done me.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}你告诉伊皮伊尼兹，就说我说的，让他去见那个刺伤了他父亲的牧人，让他原谅他，并感谢他给予我的恩惠。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{394,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
I shall tell him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}我会告诉他。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{395,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
You are to cut a lock of my hair. Say nothing to anyone, and place it on the altar\par
\ENstage{Pointing to the gate.}
in the grove of Apollo, across the road. Aglaia, after I am gone, you are to tell King Admetus that I have said that I wish him to marry again.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}你帮我剪下一束头发来，不要告诉任何人，把它放在祭坛上，{\kai （指着门）}就在路的那边，阿波罗的丛林里。阿格莱亚，等我走了之后，告诉阿德墨托斯国王，我希望他再娶一位妻子。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{396,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Queen Alcestis!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}王后啊！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{397,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
A man must have that comfort. But oh, Aglaia! They say that stepmothers often bear ill will toward the children of the former wife. Stay by them! Stay near them! And oh, Aglaia—from time to time recall me to him.\par
\ENstage{Her voice breaking.}
Recall me to him!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}一个男人需要这样的安慰。但是，阿格莱亚！人们都说继母会对前妻留下的孩子非常不好。你要陪着他们，看好他们。阿格莱亚——要时不时地，对他提起我。{\kai （快要说不下去了）}对他提起我！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{398,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
She rushes into the palace.\par
Aglaia is about to follow when she is stopped by excited whispering and talking at the gate. Some Townspeople take a few steps into the court. Rhodope, a young girl of the palace servants, smothering her happy giggling, runs from the gate to the palace door.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯跑进宫殿里。阿格莱亚正要跟上去，听到门口有人在兴高采烈地讨论着什么。一些镇上的人走进了院子，罗德普，宫里的一个年轻侍女，一边掩饰着欢笑，一边从门口跑向宫殿大门。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{399,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
What is this? What is this noise? Rhodope!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}有什么事吗？外面吵吵闹闹是怎么回事，罗德普！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{400,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Rhodope}
He's coming along the road!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{罗德普}他马上就要到了！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{401,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Who?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}谁？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{402,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Rhodope and Townspeople}
Hercules is coming! He's down the valley! Hercules, son of Zeus!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{罗德普和镇上的人们}赫拉克勒斯来了！他已经从山谷下来了！宙斯之子，赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{403,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Hush, all of you. Go out of the court. Have you forgotten that there is sickness in this house! Rhodope, go in the house and hold your tongue!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}你们都闭嘴。快从院子里出去。你们难道忘了这个家里有人生病吗？罗德普，快给我进去，闭上你的嘴！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{404,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Townspeople disappear. Rhodope slips through the palace door as it is opened by the Watchman, who enters, preceding two Servants, who are not yet seen with the day bed. Then to Aglaia, whose head is lowered in anxiety.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 镇上的人们下场。看门人打开宫殿的大门，罗德普刚好溜了进去。看门人身后跟着两个仆人，被他们抬着的卧榻挡着。他对阿格莱亚说话，而她正焦虑地低着头。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{405,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Hercules! Today of all days!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}赫拉克勒斯！大日子！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{406,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{In a bad humor.}
Yes. Yes. And every year. Why does he come every year? Do we know?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （讽刺着）}是啊，是啊，每年都来。他为什么每年都要来，为什么？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{407,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
But . . . so great a friend!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}这有什么，多尊贵的朋友！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{408,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, but great friends can sometimes make us a present of their absence.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}是啊，尊贵的朋友有时候也该为自己的缺席补上点礼物。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{409,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Aglaia, I don't understand you!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}阿格莱亚，你这是怎么回事！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{410,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Irritably.}
I know what I know.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （暴躁地）}我知道怎么回事。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{411,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
From the palace come two Servants. They are bearing a day bed and a low stool.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 两个仆人从宫殿里出来，抬着一张卧榻和一个脚凳。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{412,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
\ENstage{To the Servants.}
Come on . . . put them down here. You know where.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}{\kai （对仆人）}过来，放这儿，你们知道的。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{413,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Taking over the ordering, while the Watchman turns back to help Admetus.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 看门人转过身去扶阿德墨托斯，阿格莱亚指挥了起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{414,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Here, right here!\par
\ENstage{Getting her bearings by the sun.}
Turn it so.\par
\ENstage{She takes the cushions from them.}
So. There. And the Queen likes to sit here.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}这儿，放这儿！{\kai （照着太阳找到位置）}转过来。{\kai （从他们手里拿过垫子）}这样，对了。王后喜欢坐在这儿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{415,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Enter Admetus, supported, his arms around the shoulders of the Watchman and a Guard.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯由人搀扶着走了出来，双手分别搭在看门人和一个守卫的肩上。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{416,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
The sun is already halfway up the sky.\par
\ENstage{To the Watchman.}
You have watched all night. You should be asleep.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}已经快要正午了。{\kai （对看门人说）}你已经守了一夜，去休息吧。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{417,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Oh, we old men, King Admetus, we sleep very little. Gently. Gently now.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}啊，陛下，像我们这些老年人，睡得都很少。轻点，好一点。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{418,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I don't know where the Queen is, Aglaia.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}我没看到王后呢，阿格莱亚。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{419,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Busies herself with the cushions.}
She'll be here in a minute, King Admetus. You can be sure of that.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （忙着整理垫子）}陛下，她马上就过来。您放心吧。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{420,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
She goes quickly into the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚匆忙地走回宫中。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{421,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Now, sire, when you're comfortably settled on that couch, I have some news for you. You'll scarcely believe it.\par
\ENstage{He squints up at the sun.}
Yes, sire, it will be a very hot day. We are in the solstice. The sailors say that the sun swings low, that He is very near us these days. That's what they say.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}陛下，等您舒服地坐好了，我有些消息要告诉您。估计您都不敢相信。{\kai （斜起眼看看太阳）}是的，陛下，今天会非常热。我们已经进入夏至时节了。据海员们说，太阳现在很低，在这些日子，那个祂离我们非常近。他们就是这么说的。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{422,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
What is your news?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}所以是什么消息？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{423,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
A great friend, sire, is coming to see you—a very good friend.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}一位尊贵的朋友，陛下，要来见您。一位，好朋友。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{424,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{425,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Yes, Hercules, son of Zeus!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}是的，宙斯之子赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{426,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Today! Today of all days!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}今天！今天真是个好日子！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{427,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
The whole town knows it. Oh, he comes slowly enough. At every village, every farm, they bring him wine; they put garlands on his head. He is very drunk. . . . Oh, King Admetus, we must prevent his embracing you. You remember his embraces. He would surely kill you today.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}整个镇上都传开了，他走得也真是够慢的了。每到一个村庄，或者农场，人们都献上好酒，给他戴上花环。他呢，喝得醉醺醺的，哦，陛下，今天可不能让他拥抱您啊。您知道他那力气，还不得把您害死。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{428,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Does the Queen know?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}王后知道吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{429,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Oh, the maids will have told her all about it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}女仆们应该已经告诉她了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{430,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I wish he could have come at another time.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}他要是换个时间来就好了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{431,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Every time is a good time for Hercules. Your sons, sire, are beside themselves. Epimenes will ask him all over again how he killed the Nemean lion and how he cleaned the stables of King Augeas.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}赫拉克勒斯想什么时候来就什么时候来。陛下，小王子们都激动坏了，伊皮伊尼兹肯定又会让他讲一遍杀死复仇女神的狮子和打扫奥革阿斯牛棚的故事。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{432,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Call Aglaia.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}去把阿格莱亚叫来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{433,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Enter Aglaia from the palace door.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚从宫殿里走出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{434,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Here she is now.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}她来了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{435,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, King Admetus?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}陛下，您有什么吩咐？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{436,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Behind the King's back, with her hand she directs the Watchman to leave. He exits left.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚背着国王打手势让看门人先离开。看门人从左侧下场。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{437,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Aglaia—come near to me. You have heard the news?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}阿格莱亚，来。你听说了吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{438,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The Watchman as he leaves gives Aglaia an anxious look and is relieved by her answer.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 看门人刚要下场，很紧张地看了阿格莱亚一眼，听到她的回答后才算放心。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{439,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, I have: the son of Zeus is coming to visit us.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}听说了，宙斯之子，要来看望我们。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{440,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Without pathos.}
Aglaia, this is my last day. I know it. I feel it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （淡然地）}阿格莱亚，这是我最后的日子了。我知道，我感觉到了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{441,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Sire, how would any of us know that? You must not talk now. You must save your strength.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}陛下，这种事情没人会知道的。您还是少说几句吧。您需要保存体力。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{442,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I shall die before sundown. Now, if it is possible—and you must make it possible—my death must be hidden from Hercules, for at least a day or two. You must say that one of the servants has died. And you must go about your life as though nothing had happened. The Queen will show you the way of it. You know what Hercules's life is like—those great labors, one after another. When he comes to us as a happy visitor, we would not wish to show him a house of mourning. Especially Hercules, who is so often on the point of losing his life, and who has such an aversion to all that has to do with burial and mourning.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}太阳落山之前我就会死去。如果可行的话，我希望你能办到，拖上个一两天，不要让赫拉克勒斯得知我死亡的消息。你就说我们有个仆人死了，就像什么都没发生过一样照常生活。王后会告诉你该怎么做。你知道赫拉克勒斯一直以来的生活，总是担着沉重的工作，一个接一个。他高高兴兴地来，我不想让他看到一片哀伤。尤其是赫拉克勒斯，总是出生入死，他很讨厌葬礼啊哀哭啊这些事情。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{443,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Again arranging his cushions.}
King Admetus, I am not a fool. All these things I understand very well. Now lie back and shut your eyes. This sunlight is going to give you strength.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （又整理起垫子）}陛下，我不傻，您说的这些我都明白。您好好躺下，闭上眼睛休息，阳光会带给您力量的。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{444,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
If it could only give me strength for one more day—to welcome Hercules! Go and see where the Queen is.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}再给我一天的力气就好，好来欢迎赫拉克勒斯。你去看看王后在哪里呢。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{445,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Here she is.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}她来了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{446,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Exit Aglaia left.\par
Enter Alcestis from the palace door. She stands on the top step. She is wearing the dress of Act I. Admetus turns and gazes at her in silence.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚从左侧下场。阿尔刻提斯从宫殿里走出来。她站在最高一级的台阶上，穿着第一幕的那条裙子。阿德墨托斯回头看到她，没有说话。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{447,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Our wedding dress!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}这是咱们结婚时你穿的衣服！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{448,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Smiling, puts her finger on her lips to silence him; she goes to him and says lightly:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯微笑着，手指放在唇边示意他不要说话，并走到他身边，轻声说着。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{449,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Most wives save it for their burial. I wear it for life.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}妻子们都留着它等着葬礼的时候穿，我穿上它是为了好好活着。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{450,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Sit here, dearest. I was just saying to Aglaia—\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}亲爱的，坐这儿来。我刚还跟阿格莱亚说……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{451,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Alcestis sits on the stool beside Admetus and rests her hand beside his.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯坐在阿德墨托斯身边的凳子上，把手放在他的手边。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{452,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Yes, yes, I know—when Hercules comes . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}是的，我知道，等赫拉克勒斯来了……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{453,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
He will soon be here.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}他马上就到了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{454,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Oh, he is being detained on the road. Hercules goes quickly only to danger. Perhaps he will be here tonight . . . perhaps not till tomorrow. We have time.\par
\ENstage{She rises and goes to the center of the stage and stands there listening.}
I hear shouts of joy in the valley.\par
\ENstage{She walks slowly back to her stool and repeats:}
We have time.\par
\ENstage{In the center of the stage, gazing at the sun as if for help.}
This is the healing sun—the sun of the summer solstice. Do you feel it?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}一定是在路上耽搁了，只有危险的时候他才跑得飞快，说不定他要今晚才能到，或者要等明天了。不过我们不着急。{\kai （起身走到舞台中央，站在那里倾听着）}我听到山谷里传来的欢呼声了。{\kai （走回到凳子旁边，重复着）}我们不着急。{\kai （站在舞台中央，望着太阳仿佛要寻求帮助）}这样的阳光能够治愈人，夏至日的阳光，你感觉到了吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{455,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
It is the healing sun, but for others. I have put all that behind me: I do not need hope. My life was short, but a single hour can hold the whole fullness of time. The fullness of time was given to me. A man who has been happy is no longer the subject of time. . . . Come, we'll say to each other what is still to be said on this last day.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}这阳光能治愈人，但只能治愈别人。我已经不需要这些了，不再需要什么希望。我的一生很短暂，但短短一个钟头也足以成为完美的时光。我得到过了，最完美的时光。一个拥有过幸福的人，就不会成为时间的囚徒。来，在最后这一天，让我们把还没说完的话都好好说完。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{456,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Returning to her place beside him; with a gentle smile.}
This last day? You must rest and save your strength and breath. I shall talk. I shall talk for two—for you and for me. I do not know who gave me that second name: Alcestis the Silent. I think it was you, but also it was Hercules who carried it over Greece.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （走回他身边，温柔地微笑着）}最后这一天？你还是好好休息，留着点力气吧。听我说就好了。我来把我们两个的话都说了。我不知道是谁给我起过一个别名：沉默的阿尔刻提斯。我想应该是你，不过是赫拉克勒斯告诉了全希腊的人。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{457,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Were you always silent?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}你从前一直是沉默不语的吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{458,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
I? No. As a girl—oh, I was a contentious and argumentative girl, as you know well. But there are times when I am impatient with my silence—this tiresome, silent Alcestis! There are times when I wish to be like other women, who can freely say what is in their hearts. What do other wives say? I think they must say something like this: Admetus . . . have I ever told you—let me look into your eyes again—have I ever told you that I have loved you more than life itself?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}我？不是的，我还是个小女孩的时候，哈，我可是个很喜欢争论和反驳别人的姑娘，这你是知道的。有的时候我也会受够了自己的沉默，无聊而沉默的阿尔刻提斯！有些时候，我也想像其他的女人那样，心里想到什么就随口说什么。其他的妻子们通常会怎么说？我想她们大概都会这样说：阿德墨托斯，我有没有告诉过你——让我再好好看看你的眼睛——我有没有告诉过你，我爱你胜过我的生命？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{459,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Leaning over him, she is suddenly stricken with great pain. She rises, whirls about, one hand to her head, the other to her left side, where his wound is. Admetus has not noticed it. He has closed his eyes again, and starts to laugh in a low, long murmur.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 她正向他靠过去，一阵疼痛突然击中了她。她站了起来，有些晕眩，用右手扶着自己的头，左手则抚着阿德墨托斯的伤口。阿德墨托斯并没有注意到，他闭上眼睛，一边笑一边小声念叨着。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{460,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
That does not sound . . . no, that does not sound like my silent Alcestis. And if you were to speak for me, what would you say?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}这话听着可不像，不，听着不像我的沉默的阿尔刻提斯啊。现在轮到替我说话了，该说什么呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{461,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{The pain has slackened and she replies almost serenely.}
I would say that I—I, Admetus—have chosen neither the day of my birth nor the day of my death. That having been born is a gift that fell to our lot—a wonderful gift—and that Alcestis's death—or my own—comes from the same hand.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （疼痛减轻了一些，她静静地说）}我会说，我，阿德墨托斯，无法选择自己的出生和死亡。生命是神明所赐的礼物，一份神奇的礼物，而赐下生命的神明，也会赐予阿尔刻提斯，或是我死亡。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{462,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Almost amused.}
Your death, Alcestis?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （有点惊奇）}你的死亡，阿尔刻提斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{463,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Were it to come from the same hand that gives life . . . Are you in pain, Admetus?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}难道不是同一种力量吗，赐予生命的也……你哪里不舒服吗，阿德墨托斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{464,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Puts his hand to his side wonderingly.}
No. I do not know why it is, but the pain seems to have . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （很惊讶地把手放到一边）}没有，我不知道为什么，不过我的疼痛好像……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{465,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Lift your hand!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}举起手试试！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{466,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
As he slowly raises his paralyzed left hand, the pain passes into her body; she clutches her side, bending in agony.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他缓缓地举起那只不能动弹的左手，痛苦转移到了阿尔刻提斯的身上，她抓着衣服，痛苦地弯下了腰。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{467,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
This lightness!\par
\ENstage{He stares before him with hope.}
No, I must not think of such things. I have put that behind me. We who have known what we have known . . . are not the subjects of time.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}这光芒！{\kai （他盯着自己的前方，有了希望）}不，我不该想这些事情了。我已经不需要这些了，我们已经品尝了生命的富足，我们已经不是时间的囚徒了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{468,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The great pain has passed, but she moves guardedly; she has sat down again and speaks with her cheek pressed close to his.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 剧烈的疼痛过去了，阿尔刻提斯小心翼翼地动弹一下，又坐了下来，脸贴脸地对阿德墨托斯说话。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{469,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Yes, we, who were happy . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}是啊，我们，有过那么多快乐。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{470,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Suddenly grasping her hand, ardently.}
And you hated me once!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （突然抓住了她的手，激动地）}你之前讨厌我！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{471,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Withdrawing her hand.}
No, never.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （把手抽出来）}没有，从来没有。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{472,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
That young man who kept coming back to that trial of the lion and the boar . . . There he is again—that young idiot from Thessaly.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}有个年轻人，一而再地去参加那场狮子与野猪的比赛……他又来了——那个塞萨利的大傻瓜。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{473,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
They are both laughing.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 两人都笑了起来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{474,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Oh, what a road I have come!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}啊，人生多奇妙啊！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{475,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
You didn't hate me, as I came around the corner straining over those damned beasts?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}你不讨厌我吗，当我驾着那两只该死的野兽绕过城墙的时候？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{476,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
