No. I suffered the more for it. I had begun to love that stern-faced young man from Thessaly. You were the only suitor who attempted that trial twice. . . . Even Hercules gave up. . . . To think that I could not see where life was carrying me!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}不，我当时更受煎熬啊。因为我已经爱上了那个塞萨利来的，一脸严肃的年轻人。你是唯一一个两次来参加比试的追求者。连赫拉克勒斯都放弃了，当时，我不知道我的人生将会怎样。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{477,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Proudly, ardently.}
I, I saw.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （骄傲地，激动地）}我，我知道。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{478,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Beloved Admetus . . . you saw. You married this self-willed, obstinate girl.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}亲爱的阿德墨托斯……你知道，你娶了一个自私又倔强的女孩。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{479,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
She makes gestures of trying to silence him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿尔刻提斯做了个手势示意他不要再说下去。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{480,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{More ardently.}
Our love! Our love! . . . Our whisperings in the night! . . . The birth of Epimenes, when I almost lost you . . .\par
Alcestis! What we have known, what we have lived . . . Oh, to live forever—with you—beside you.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （更加热烈）}我们的爱！我们之间的爱！我们在夜晚说的那些悄悄话！伊皮伊尼兹出生的时候，我差点就失去你了。阿尔刻提斯！这所有的一切，人生啊……我真想一直这样活下去，和你一起，在你身边。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{481,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Ssh! There are things that we human beings are not permitted to say aloud.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}嘘！这些话我们人类可不能大声说出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{482,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Tentatively putting his foot on the ground.}
I do not understand. . . . My knee does not tremble.\par
\ENstage{With joyous hope.}
Alcestis! It may be . . . it may be I shall live.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （试着让双脚落地）}我不知道怎么了……我的膝盖竟然没有在发抖了。{\kai （充满着喜悦的希望）}阿尔刻提斯！也许，也许我真的会活下去！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{483,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Equally ecstatic, to the sky.}
Living or dead, we are watched; we are guided; we are understood. Oh, Admetus, lie quiet, lie still!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （带着同样的喜悦，望着天空）}无论是死，是生，我们都被眷顾着，指引着，懂得着。哦，阿德墨托斯，静静地躺在那里吧，静静地。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{484,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I dare not . . . believe . . . hope . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}我简直不敢……相信，不敢去奢望……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{485,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{The pain returns; she is starting toward the palace door.}
Admetus, I would find it a natural thing, if a message came from Delphi to me, saying that I should give my life for my children or for Thessaly or . . . for my husband . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （疼痛再次袭击了她，她向宫殿走去）}阿德墨托斯，如果德尔斐有人送来消息给我，说要让我为了我的孩子，或是塞萨利，或是——我的丈夫而死，我不会犹豫。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{486,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
No. No. No man would wish another to die for him. Every man is ready to die his own death.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}不，不，没有人希望别人为自己而死。每一个人都要承受他自己的死亡。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{487,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Mastering her suffering.}
What are you saying, Admetus? Think of all the soldiers—thousands and thousands of them—who have died for others. And we women, poor cowardly soldiers, have died—a great many of us—for our husbands and children.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （掩藏着自己的疼痛）}你说的是什么话呀，阿德墨托斯！想想那些战士们——成千上万的战士们——都是为他人而死的啊。我们这些女人，这些可怜又怯懦的战士，许许多多也都为了自己的丈夫和孩子们而死去。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{488,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
She starts stumbling toward the palace door.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 她踉踉跄跄地走向宫殿大门。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{489,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I would think less of the gods who could lay such a decision between husband and wife. . . .\par
\ENstage{Rising; in loud amazement.}
Look! This lightness!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}如果是神明们决意让妻子为了丈夫去死，我会很看不起他们的。{\kai （起身，惊讶地呼喊着）}你看！那光芒！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{490,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Aglaia! Aglaia!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}阿格莱亚！阿格莱亚！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{491,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Springs up and rushes to her.}
Alcestis, you are in trouble! Aglaia! Aglaia!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （起身跑向她）}阿尔刻提斯，你看起来不太对劲！阿格莱亚！阿格莱亚！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{492,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Aglaia hurries to her from the palace door.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚从宫门出来跑向她。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{493,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
Take me to my bed.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}把我扶到床上去。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{494,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
You are ill. Are you ill, Alcestis?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}你生病了，你是不是病了，阿尔刻提斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{495,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Alcestis}
\ENstage{Turning.}
Take my life. Be happy. Be happy.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿尔刻提斯}{\kai （转过脸）}我把我的生命给你。要快乐，要快乐。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{496,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
She collapses in their arms and is carried into the palace. From beyond the gate come sounds of an excited crowd, fragments of singing, etc., and the roar of Hercules's voice.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 她晕倒在他们的臂弯，大家把她抬进了宫殿。右边门口传来人群的欢呼声，断断续续的歌声，接着就是赫拉克勒斯洪亮的嗓音。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{497,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Where is my old friend Ad-meeee-tus?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我的好哥们儿阿德墨——托斯在哪里？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{498,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Townspeople}
Hercules is here! Hercules is here!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{居民}赫拉克勒斯来啦！赫拉克勒斯来啦！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{499,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Al-cess-s-stis! Where is the divine Al-cess-stis? Alcestis the Silent! Admetus the Hoss-s-spitable!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}阿尔刻——提斯！圣洁的阿尔刻——提斯在哪里？沉默的阿尔刻提斯！热情诚——挚的阿德墨托斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{500,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The Watchman enters.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 看门人走了进来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{501,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Townspeople}
Hercules is here! Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{居民}赫拉克勒斯来啦！赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{502,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
The gods preserve us! What can be done?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}神明保佑！现在可怎么办？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{503,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
More Townspeople pour in the gate, shouting, ``Long live Hercules, son of Zeus!'' Enter Hercules—drunk, happy, garlanded—a jug in his hand. Two Villagers laughingly carry Hercules's club as a great burden.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 镇上的人不断从小门涌进来，一边还叫喊着：“宙斯之子赫拉克勒斯万岁！”赫拉克勒斯上场——醉醺醺的，兴高采烈的，头上戴着花环，手里拿着酒壶。两个村民大笑着，抬着赫拉克勒斯沉甸甸的大棍子。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{504,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Alcestis, fairest of the daughters of Iolcos! Admetus, crown of friends! Where are they?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}阿尔刻提斯，伊俄尔科斯最美的女儿！阿德墨托斯，我最最亲爱的朋友！他们都在哪儿！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{505,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Townspeople}
Hercules, the destroyer of beasts! Hercules, the friend of man! Long live Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{居民}赫拉克勒斯，猛兽的毁灭者！赫拉克勒斯，人类的好朋友！赫拉克勒斯万岁！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{506,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Welcome a thousand times to Pherai, great Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}伟大的赫拉克勒斯，对于您的到来，费莱致以一千次的欢迎！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{507,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Where is my friend Epimenes—the mighty hunter, the mighty fisherman? Epimenes, I shall wrestle with you. By the God's thunder, you shall not throw me again!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我的好朋友伊皮伊尼兹在哪里，勇敢的猎手，能干的渔夫？伊皮伊尼兹，我要跟你比试摔跤。我以神的雷电起誓，这次可不会再让你摔我一大跤了！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{508,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
All of them will be here in a minute, Hercules. They are beside themselves with joy.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}他们马上就过来，赫拉克勒斯。他们全都高兴坏了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{509,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
A wailing, a keening, is heard in the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 宫殿里传来一阵哀号和哭喊。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{510,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
That's weeping I hear. What is that wailing, old man?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我听到有哭声。老人家，他们在哭什么？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{511,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Watchman}
Wailing, Hercules! The women and girls are rejoicing that Hercules has come.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{看门人}您说哭声，赫拉克勒斯！那是妇人和姑娘们在为了您的到来欢呼尖叫呢。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{512,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Enter Aglaia in haste from the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚从宫殿里匆匆走出来。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{513,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
A thousand times welcome, divine Hercules. The King and Queen will be here in a moment. Happy, happy they are—you can be sure of that. Oh, son of Zeus, what a joy to see you!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}神圣的赫拉克勒斯，向您致以一千次的欢迎。国王和王后马上就来，他们太……太开心了，您知道的。哦，宙斯之子，见到您是多么荣幸啊。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{514,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Loudly.}
I'm not the son of Zeus!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （大声地）}我不是什么宙斯之子！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{515,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Covering her ears.}
Hercules, what are you saying?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （捂上耳朵）}赫拉克勒斯，您在乱说什么呀？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{516,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
I'm the son of Amphytrion and Alcmene. I'm a common man, Aglaia; and the work I do is as hard for me as for any other.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我是安菲特律翁和阿尔克墨涅的儿子。我是一个普通人，阿格莱亚。我做起事情来和别人一样费力。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{517,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Oh, may the gods prevent misfortune from coming to this house! You're very drunk, Hercules, to say such a wicked thing as that.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}哦，愿众神不要降灾给这个家！您居然说出这么坏的话来，赫拉克勒斯，您一定是喝醉了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{518,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
I'm a man, just an ordinary man, I tell you.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我告诉你，我只是一个人，一个普通的人。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{519,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
God or man, Hercules, what do I see? Hercules, you are filthy! Is this the handsomest man in all Greece? By the immortal gods, I would never have known you! Now listen: you remember the baths that Aglaia prepares? They'd take the skin off an ordinary man. And the oil I have ready for you—you remember the oil, don't you?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}神也好，人也好，赫拉克勒斯，让我瞧瞧，您怎么脏成这样了！这还是我们全希腊最帅的男人吗？凭着不朽的众神起誓，我都快认不出您来了！现在听好了，您还记得阿格莱亚总是为您准备的热水澡吗？他们会把您这“普通人”的样子给洗掉的。我为您准备了精油，还记得那些精油吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{520,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Confidentially.}
First, Aglaia—first!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （自信地）}首先，阿格莱亚，首先！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{521,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He makes a gesture of drinking.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他做了一个喝酒的姿势。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{522,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
You haven't forgotten our wine, is that it? You shall have some immediately!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}您还没忘了我们的好酒，是不是？马上就准备！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{523,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Suddenly roaring.}
On the road they told me that Admetus had been stabbed and wounded. Who struck him, Aglaia?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （突然吼了起来）}我在来的路上听说阿德墨托斯被人刺伤了。谁伤害的他，阿格莱亚？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{524,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Oh, that's all forgotten, Hercules. He's as well now as you or I!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}哦，这事儿已经过去了，赫拉克勒斯。他已经好起来了，跟你我一样。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{525,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Rhodope and another Girl come from the palace bearing a wine jar and some cups.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 罗德普和另一个姑娘从宫里走出来，扛着酒罐，拿着几个杯子。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{526,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup Come, sit here and refresh yourself.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 过来，坐这儿，好好放松一下。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{527,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Hercules tries to catch one of the girls, who eludes him. He then starts in pursuit of the other. He catches her.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 赫拉克勒斯想抓住其中一个女孩，女孩躲开了。他转而去追另一个，一把抓住了她。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{528,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
What's your name, little pigeon?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}你叫什么名字，我的小心肝？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{529,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Angrily.}
Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （生气地）}赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{530,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
The Girl escapes. Hercules runs after her, stumbles, and falls flat on the ground.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 女孩跑掉，赫拉克勒斯追上去，跌了一跤，摔倒在地上。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{531,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Oh! I've hurt myself! Hell and confusion! My knee! My knee!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}哦！我受伤了！真他妈的混账！我的膝盖！我的膝盖！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{532,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Hercules! I haven't one bit of pity for you. Can you have forgotten where you are? Immortal heavens, what would Queen Alcestis think—you behaving like that!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}赫拉克勒斯！我可一点都不同情您。您忘了这是哪儿了吗？不朽的天神啊，阿尔刻提斯王后要是看到您这副模样，该怎么想您啊！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{533,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Who has slowly got up and sat down.}
Twenty days I've walked. Where are they? Where are my friends, Admetus and Alcestis?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （慢慢起身，坐下）}我都走了二十天了。他们人呢？我的朋友们，阿德墨托斯和阿尔刻提斯呢？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{534,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Confidentially.}
Now, Hercules, you're an old friend of the house, aren't you?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （严肃地）}好了，赫拉克勒斯，您是这个家的老朋友了，是不是？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{535,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
I am!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}当然是了！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{536,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
We can talk quite plainly to you, can't we? You're not one of those guests we have to conceal things from, thank the gods!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}我们有什么就可以说什么，对不对？感谢神明，我们不需要像对其他客人一样对您掩藏什么。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{537,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
I'm their brother! Their brother!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我就是他们俩的兄长！哥哥！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{538,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Now listen: one of the women in the house, an orphan . . . one of the women in the house . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}我要跟您说的是，这个家里有一位夫人，一个孤儿……这个家有位夫人……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{539,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
What? Dead?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}怎么了？死了？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{540,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Finger on lips.}
And you know how loyal and kind King Admetus and Queen Alcestis are to all of us who serve here. . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （手指放在唇间）}您也知道国王和王后对我们这些仆人非常好……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{541,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Dead? An orphan?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}死了？一个孤儿？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{542,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, she'd lost both her father and mother. Now, they'll be here in just a minute—after you've had your bath. But now, just now—you know: they are by that poor girl, in friendship and piety. You understand everything, Hercules?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}是的，她没有双亲。好了，他们应该很快就到了，等您沐浴过后。但是现在，此刻，您知道，他们像朋友一样，充满怜悯地陪着那个可怜的姑娘。这些事情您都很明白的，对吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{543,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
No. No, Aglaia, there are too many things I do not understand. But do you know who understands them all?\par
\ENstage{Pause.}
Alcestis. Am I not right, Aglaia?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}不，不，阿格莱亚，很多事情我都不明白。但谁又全都明白呢？{\kai （停顿）}阿尔刻提斯明白，对不对，阿格莱亚？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{544,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes. Yes, Hercules.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}是的，是的，赫拉克勒斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{545,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Now I am going to talk frankly to you, Aglaia: I have come here—I have walked twenty days—to ask Alcestis one question.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}那我也对你实话实说吧，阿格莱亚。我来这儿——走了二十天来这儿——是要问阿尔刻提斯一个问题。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{546,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
A question, Hercules?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}一个问题，赫拉克勒斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{547,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
For answer, Hercules twice points quickly and emphatically toward the zenith.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 赫拉克勒斯两次指了指天空暗示自己的问题。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{548,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
About the gods?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}关于，众神？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{549,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He nods. Aglaia recovers herself and says briskly:\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他点点头，阿格莱亚回过神来，干脆地说。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{550,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Well, first you must have that bath. You will come out of it looking like a boy of seventeen. And I have such a garland for you. And such perfumes!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}好吧，不过您还是要先去沐浴。洗完澡之后，您看起来会像个十七岁的小伙子。我还要给您一顶漂亮的花环，还有香水！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{551,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He rises and starts to follow her to the palace steps.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 赫拉克勒斯起身，跟在她身后登上宫殿的台阶。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{552,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
And while I'm taking the bath you will sit beside me and tell me again how Apollo came to Thessaly?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我洗澡的时候，你再给我讲讲阿波罗到塞萨利的事情吧？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{553,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Yes, I will. I'll tell you once more.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}好的，没问题，我再给您讲一遍。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{554,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Stopping her; with urgency.}
And no one knew which was Apollo?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （突然打断了她）}一直没人搞清楚哪个是阿波罗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{555,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
She shakes her head.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚摇摇头。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{556,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup A whole year, and no one knew which was Apollo—not even Alcestis?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 一整年时间，没人搞清楚哪个是阿波罗，包括阿尔刻提斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{557,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Again she shakes her head. Hercules clutches his forehead.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 她又摇了摇头，赫拉克勒斯拍拍自己的脑门。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{558,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup Aglaia, who can understand them? We shall never understand them. When I try to think of them, I start trembling; I get dizzy.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 阿格莱亚，谁能搞明白他们？我们估计永远搞不明白。我每次一想起他们啊，我就开始发抖，头晕。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{559,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Hercules! You are tired. Come. . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}赫拉克勒斯！您太累了，跟我来……\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{560,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Enter Admetus. He stands at the top of the stairs as Hercules and Aglaia reach the bottom step.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯走了进来，赫拉克勒斯和阿格莱亚准备上台阶时，他正好站在最上面一级台阶上。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{561,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Loudly.}
Welcome, Hercules, friend of all men; Hercules, benefactor of all men!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （大声地）}欢迎你，赫拉克勒斯，人类的朋友。赫拉克勒斯，人类的大恩人！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{562,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Admetus! Old friend!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}阿德墨托斯！我的老朋友！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{563,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Admetus puts his hands on Hercules's shoulders. They gaze into one another's eyes.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯把双手放在赫拉克勒斯的肩上，他们互相盯着对方的眼睛看了好一阵子。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{564,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
From where have you come, Hercules?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}你从哪里来的，赫拉克勒斯？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{565,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
From labors, Admetus . . . from labors.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}从劳苦重担中来，阿德墨托斯……从劳苦重担中。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{566,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
King Admetus, Hercules's bath is ready. You can begin talking when he comes out.\par
\ENstage{Looking up.}
It will be evening soon.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}阿德墨托斯国王，赫拉克勒斯正准备去沐浴。您可以等他待会儿出来了再聊。{\kai （看看天空）}天色马上就晚了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{567,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
No, first we will drink a bowl of wine together! Come, sit down, Hercules. For a short time I was ill, and this foolish womanish couch is where I used to sit in the sun. Now tell me—what has been your latest great labor?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}不行，我们先要一起喝上一碗！过来，坐下，赫拉克勒斯。我病了一段时间，这个又呆又傻的沙发，我之前总是坐在这儿晒太阳。现在跟我说说，你最近在干什么大事。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{568,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Aglaia and the Girls have gone into the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚和女孩们都回到宫里。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{569,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Hushed, wide-eyed.}
Admetus, Admetus—I killed the Hydra.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （急切地，睁大了眼睛）}阿德墨托斯，阿德墨托斯——我杀了九头蛇。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{570,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Rising, in awed astonishment.}
Great-hearted Hercules! You slew the Hydra! By the immortal gods, Hercules, you are the friend of man. Slew the Hydra! The Hydra!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （站起身，十分惊讶）}非凡的赫拉克勒斯！你杀了九头蛇！凭着不朽的神明起誓，赫拉克勒斯，你真是人类的好朋友。你杀了九头蛇！九头蛇啊！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{571,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Beckoning to Admetus to draw his face nearer for a confidence.}
Admetus!\par
\ENstage{Again beckoning.}
It wasn't easy!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （指了指自己，让阿德墨托斯靠得更近）}阿德墨托斯！{\kai （又指了指自己）}很不容易！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{572,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
I can well believe it!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}我简直不敢相信！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{573,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Almost bitterly; gazing into his face.}
It was not easy.\par
\ENstage{Abrupt change to urgent earnestness.}
Admetus, am I the son of Zeus?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （盯着他的脸，简直有些苦涩）}很不容易。{\kai （忽然变得十分恳切）}阿德墨托斯，我是宙斯之子吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{574,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Hercules! Everyone knows that you are the son of Zeus and Alcmene.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}赫拉克勒斯！所有人都知道，你是宙斯和阿尔克墨涅的儿子。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{575,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
One person can tell me. That is what I have come to ask Alcestis. Where is Alcestis, queen of women?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}有一个人能告诉我答案。我来这儿就是要问阿尔刻提斯这件事。阿尔刻提斯在哪儿？真正的女王在哪儿？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{576,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
But Aglaia told you!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}阿格莱亚不是跟你说了吗！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{577,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Oh, yes. Who is this orphan girl that has died? Was she one of the first in the house?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}哦，对。那个死去的孤儿是谁？是这个家最初的那批人吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{578,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
She called herself the servant of the servants.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}她称自己为仆人中的仆人。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{579,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
But you were all fond of her?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}你们都很喜欢她是吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{580,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Yes. We all loved her.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}是的，我们都很爱她。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{581,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
You see, Admetus, everyone says I am the son of Zeus and therefore my labors must be easier for me than for another man. If I have the blood and the heart and the lungs of Him\par
\ENstage{Pointing upward.}
in me, shouldn't they be easy? But, Admetus, they are not easy. The Hydra!\par
\ENstage{He wraps his arms around Admetus in imitation of a snake and sets his face in extreme horror.}
I was about to burst. The blood sprang out of my ears like fountains. If I am only a man—the son of Amphytrion and Alcmene—then, Admetus,\par
\ENstage{Peering into Admetus's face with strained urgency.}
then I'm a very good man!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}你瞧，阿德墨托斯，人人都说我是宙斯之子，所以我办起事情来应该比别人容易很多。如果我拥有跟他一样的血液、心脏、肺腑{\kai （指着天空）}，事情不就应该很容易吗？但是啊，阿德墨托斯，很不容易。九头蛇！{\kai （用手臂把阿德墨托斯环起来，模仿着九头蛇做出恐怖的表情）}我当时快要炸开了，鲜血跟泉水一样从我耳朵里喷出来。如果我只是一个人——安菲特律翁和阿尔克墨涅的儿子——那么，阿德墨托斯，{\kai （用力戳了戳阿德墨托斯的脸）}我肯定是个非常了不起的凡人！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{582,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
God or man, Hercules—god or man, all men honor and are grateful to you.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}神也好，人也好，赫拉克勒斯——是神也好，人也好，所有人都会敬重你，感激你。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{583,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
But I want to know. Some days I feel that I am the son of Zeus. Other days I am . . . I am a beast, Admetus, a beast and a brute. Every month messengers arrive from all over Greece, asking me to come and do this and do that. I'll do no more; I won't do a single thing until I've settled this matter. One of these days—yes, I foresee it—someone will come to me and ask me to . . . descend into the underworld . . . into Hell, Admetus, and bring back someone who has died.\par
\ENstage{Rising, in terrified repudiation.}
No! No! That I will not do. God or man, no one may ask that of me!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}但是，我想弄明白。有时候我觉得我就是宙斯之子，但有时候我又……又是一只野兽。阿德墨托斯，我觉得自己野蛮而残暴。每个月，信使们从希腊各地送来消息，请我去干这个干那个。我什么都不干了，在我解决这件事情之前，我什么事都不会再干了。就这几天，我已经预见到了，有人会来找我，请我去……去下面的世界，去冥界。阿德墨托斯，去把一个已死的人带回来。{\kai （站起来，带着惊恐拒绝道）}不！不行！这事我不会干！神也好，人也好，谁都别指望我干这事！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{584,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Sincerely shocked.}
No, Hercules—that has never been done, nor even thought of.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （被震惊了）}不，赫拉克勒斯——这事没人干过，也没人想过。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{585,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Every time I come here I look at that—that entrance to Hell—with great fear, Admetus.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我每次到这儿来都会看到它——冥界的入口——我非常害怕，阿德墨托斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{586,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
You must not think such thoughts!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}你不要再想这些事情了！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{587,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Even a son of Zeus couldn't do that, could he? So—so do you see the question? Isn't that an important question? And who can answer it? Who is wisest in just these matters where you and I are most ignorant? You know who: she who never speaks, or speaks so little. The Silent. She is silent, isn't she?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}就算是宙斯之子也做不到这件事，对吗？所以，你明白问题是什么吗？这难道不是个很要命的问题吗？谁能回答呢？在这种你我都很无知的问题上谁最有智慧呢？你知道是谁，就是那个总是沉默，少言寡语的人。沉默，她总是沉默着，对吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{588,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Yes, she is silent.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}是的，她很沉默。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{589,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
But she'll speak to me . . . to her old friend Hercules? She'll speak for me, won't she?\par
\ENstage{He pours himself more wine.}
Do you know, Admetus—do you know what your Alcestis is?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}但她会跟我说话的……会跟她的老朋友赫拉克勒斯说话吧？她也会为我说话的，对吗？{\kai （灌一大口酒）}你知道吗，阿德墨托斯，你了解你的阿尔刻提斯是什么样的吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{590,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Admetus, in pain, rises, and sits down.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿德墨托斯痛苦地站了起来，又坐下。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{591,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
No, you don't know. I journey from place to place—from court to court. I've seen them all—the queens and princesses of Greece. The daughters of the men we knew and the girls we courted are growing up. Oedipus of Thebes has a daughter, Antigone; and there is Penelope, who's just married the son of Laertes of Ithaca. Leda of Sparta has two daughters, Helen and Clytemnestra. I've seen 'em all, talked to 'em all. What are they beside Alcestis? Dirt. Trash.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}不，你不知道。我去过很多地方，也追求过很多人。希腊的这些王后啊，公主啊，我见过太多了。我们认识的这些大人物的女儿，我们追求过的姑娘都长大了。忒拜的俄狄浦斯有个女儿，安提戈涅。还有佩涅洛佩，不久前嫁给了伊萨卡的拉厄耳忒斯的儿子。斯巴达的勒达有两个女儿，海伦和克吕泰涅斯特拉。我见过她们每个人，也和她们都聊过天。可是在阿尔刻提斯面前她们算什么呢？尘土，垃圾。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{592,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Aglaia comes from the palace bearing a garland of vine leaves. The Watchman follows with a jar of oil. They stand listening.\par
Hercules rises and, in drunken energy close to violence, turns Admetus about, shouting.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 阿格莱亚从宫殿里出来，手里拿着藤蔓编织的花环。看门人跟在后面，端着一罐精油。他们站在旁边聆听着。赫拉克勒斯起身，醉醺醺的要发狂。他转向阿德墨托斯，大喊着。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{593,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Do you know? Do you know to the full?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}你了解吗？你了解她的全部吗？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{594,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{In agony, raising his arms.}
I know, Hercules.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （痛苦地抬起手臂）}我了解，赫拉克勒斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{595,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Do you know all—her power to forgive, to pardon? No, you don't know a thing. Alcestis is the crown of Greece. The crown of women.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}你了解她的全部吗？她宽恕、原谅的能力？不，你什么都不知道。阿尔刻提斯才是希腊的珍宝，女人中的珍宝。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{596,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Taking three steps backward.}
Hercules! Alcestis is dead.\par
\ENstage{Pause.}
The servant of servants. Forgive us, Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （往后退了三步）}赫拉克勒斯！阿尔刻提斯死了。{\kai （停顿）}仆人中的仆人。原谅我，赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{597,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Silence. A great rage rises in him. Then with savage bitterness.}
Admetus . . . you are no friend. By the immortal gods, you are my worst enemy!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （沉默了一阵。一股巨大的愤怒从心中升起，极其痛苦地说）}阿德墨托斯……你算什么朋友。凭着不朽的天神起誓，你是我最恨的敌人！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{598,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
He grasps him by the throat and, walking backward, drags the unresisting Admetus with him.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 他一把扼住阿德墨托斯的咽喉，拖着丝毫没有抵抗的阿德墨托斯向后退了几步。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{599,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup If you were not Admetus, I would kill you now!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup 你要不是阿德墨托斯的话，我现在就杀了你！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{600,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia and Watchman}
Hercules! Hercules, Queen Alcestis wished it! She wanted us not to tell you until later.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚和看门人}赫拉克勒斯，赫拉克勒斯！是阿尔刻提斯王后要求这么做的！她让我们晚一些再告诉您！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{601,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
You have treated me as no friend. I thought I was your brother.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}你们就没把我当朋友！我还以为我们是兄弟！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{602,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
\ENstage{Pounding with her fists on his back.}
Hercules! Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}{\kai （一直用拳头敲打赫拉克勒斯的后背）}赫拉克勒斯！赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{603,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Alcestis dead—and I was not worthy to be told! You let me boast and drink and revel . . .\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}阿尔刻提斯死了，你们觉得用不着告诉我！你们还让我在这儿吹牛，喝酒，瞎胡闹！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{604,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
It was her wish, Hercules. Alcestis commanded it.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}是她吩咐的，赫拉克勒斯。阿尔刻提斯下令我们这么做。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{605,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Broodingly, holding Admetus bent far backward before him.}
You think I have no mind or heart or soul. What do I care for the thanks and praise of the world if I am not fit to share the grief of my friends?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （沉思着，推着阿德墨托斯向后走了好几步）}你们觉得我没有脑子，没有心肝，没有灵魂吗？我连为朋友分担悲伤的资格都没有，就算有全世界的感谢和赞美有什么用？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{606,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
Pause.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 停顿。\par }
