\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{607,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
It was for hospitality, Hercules. She wished it!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}只是为了看起来热情一些，赫拉克勒斯。她希望如此！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{608,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Hospitality is for guests, not brothers.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}客人才需要热情，真正的兄弟不需要。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{609,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
\ENstage{Quietly.}
Forgive us, Hercules.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}{\kai （安静地）}请原谅我们，赫拉克勒斯。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{610,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
\ENstage{Releasing Admetus.}
Alcestis is dead. Alcestis is in the underworld.\par
\ENstage{Suddenly struck by an idea; loudly.}
In Hell! Where is my club? My club! I shall go and get her!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}{\kai （放开阿德墨托斯）}阿尔刻提斯死了。阿尔刻提斯已经在另一个世界了。{\kai （突然想到了什么，大声地）}她在冥界！我的棍子呢？我的棍子！我要去把她救出来！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{611,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Hercules!\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}赫拉克勒斯！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{612,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
No, Hercules. Live! She died in my place. She died for me. It is not right that still another die.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}不，赫拉克勒斯。你得活着！她是代替我死的，她是为我而死的。不能再有人为此送命了。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{613,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
My club! My club! Admetus, now I shall tell you something—something that no man knows. Now all the world may know it.\par
\ENstage{He has advanced to the top of the path.}
I once came near to Alcestis in violence, in brutish violence. Yes, I, Hercules, son of Zeus, did that! A god—some god—intervened in time, to save her and to save me. Alcestis forgave me. How can that be? How can any man understand that? She spoke of it to no one in the world. And when I came to Pherai there was no sign in her face, in her eyes, that I had been the criminal. Only a god can understand that—only that loving smile. Forgiveness is not within our power—we commoner men. Only the strong and pure can forgive. I never wanted that she should forget that evil moment—no!—for in her remembering it lay my happiness; for her remembering and her forgiving were one.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}我的棍子！我的棍子！阿德墨托斯，现在我要告诉你一件事——我从没告诉过任何人。现在，大家都应该知道。{\kai （向前走到小路的高处）}我曾经差点强暴了阿尔刻提斯，极其粗野。是的，我，赫拉克勒斯，宙斯之子，做了那样的事情！但是有一位神明——某位神明——及时介入进来，救了她也救了我。阿尔刻提斯原谅了我。这怎么可能？有谁能理解呢？她没有对任何人提起过这件事。我再次来到费莱时，在她的表情中、眼神里看不到任何我曾经犯过罪的痕迹。只有神明才能够理解，只有那样的微笑可以解释。宽恕不是我们这些凡人能够做到的。只有坚强的、圣洁的人才有能力宽恕。我甚至希望她不要忘记那个邪恶的瞬间，不要。因为能被她想起是我的幸福，因为她想起我时，就已经宽恕了我。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{614,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
Hercules, I shall come with you.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}赫拉克勒斯，我和你一起去！\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{615,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Stay and rule, Admetus. Your labors I cannot do; you cannot do mine. What god shall I call on? Not my father—father and no father. Who is the god you mainly worship here?\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}留下来好好治理国家，阿德墨托斯。你的工作我做不了，我的你也做不了。我能指望哪个神明呢？肯定不会是我父亲——有和没有一个样。你们在这儿都是崇拜哪一位神明？\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{616,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Aglaia}
Have you forgotten, Hercules? This is Apollo's land.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿格莱亚}你忘了吗，赫拉克勒斯。这是阿波罗的领地啊。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{617,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Yes, now I remember. I have had little to do with Him, but . . .\par
\ENstage{Looking to heaven.}
Apollo, I am Hercules, called the son of Zeus and Alcmene. All Greece says that you have loved these two—Admetus and Alcestis. You know what I'm about to do. You know I can't do it by myself. Put into my arm a strength that's never been there before. You do this—or let's say, you and I do this together. And if we can do it, let everybody see that a new knowledge has been given to us of what gods and men can do together.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}是，我想起来了。我和他没怎么打过交道，不过……{\kai （望着天空）}阿波罗，我是赫拉克勒斯，传说中宙斯和阿尔克墨涅的儿子。整个希腊都知道您一直爱着这两位——阿德墨托斯和阿尔刻提斯。您知道我打算做什么，您也知道我一个人做不到。请赐予我从未有过的力量吧，请您来完成，或者说，您和我一起完成这件事。如果我们能做到的话，人们就会拥有全新的认识，他们会看到神明和人类合作起来可以做到什么。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{618,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Admetus}
One moment, Hercules.\par
\ENstage{He goes down the path.}
Pytho! Pytho! This is Hercules, the loved friend of Alcestis and of me and of Epimenes. Let him pass, Pytho—going and coming.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{阿德墨托斯}请等一下，赫拉克勒斯。{\kai （沿着小道走下来）}佩索！佩索！这是赫拉克勒斯，这是阿尔刻提斯和我，还有伊皮伊尼兹最亲爱的朋友。请让他从这里通过，佩索——从这里去而复返。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{619,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \ENspeaker{Hercules}
Go into the palace, Admetus—all of you. I must work as I must live—alone.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \ZHspeaker{赫拉克勒斯}回宫殿里去吧，阿德墨托斯，你们都回去吧。我必须独自做工，如同必须独自生活。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{620,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\setbox\enbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ENsetup \itshape 
It is dark. The characters on the stage withdraw, except for Admetus, who stands by the palace door, covering his face with his cloak. A low beating on tympani, like distant thunder, grows louder, strikes a sharp blow, and jumps up a fourth. Hercules has disappeared into the door of the cave. Presently he returns, leading Alcestis. Over her white dress and head she wears a dark veil, which trails many yards behind her. As they reach the stage, Hercules releases her hand. She sways, with groping steps, as though drugged with sleep. Hercules shoulders his club and goes off in his solitary way. Admetus, holding his cloak before him, as before a strong light, approaches, enfolds her. She rests her head on his breast. He leads her into the palace.\par}
\setbox\enfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\setbox\zhbox=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\ZHsetup \kai 场上一片黑暗，角色们纷纷退场，除了阿德墨托斯还站在宫殿门口，用斗篷挡着脸。低沉的鼓声响起，像是远方传来的雷声。声渐响，鼓点急切且跳跃。赫拉克勒斯从洞穴的入口处消失，片刻后他再次出现，领着阿尔刻提斯。她的全身覆着深色的纱巾，在身后拖了很长。快要登上舞台时，赫拉克勒斯松开了手。她晃晃悠悠地摸索着向前走，像是在梦游。赫拉克勒斯夹起自己的棍子，独自一人离开。阿德墨托斯像是要避开眼前的强光一样用斗篷遮住自己。他慢慢靠近，揭开阿尔刻提斯的面纱。她把头靠在他的胸前，他将她带回宫殿。\par }
\setbox\zhfoot=\vbox{\hsize=119mm\normalfont\fontsize{7.5}{11}\selectfont }
\immediate\write\metrics{621,\number\dimexpr\ht\enbox+\dp\enbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhbox+\dp\zhbox\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\enfoot+\dp\enfoot\relax,\number\dimexpr\ht\zhfoot+\dp\zhfoot\relax}
\immediate\closeout\metrics
\null
\end{document}
